    [39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
        (function () {\n
            /* Every class the shell puts on [39m[31m<html>[39m[31m before paint, in one function that both ends of\n
               the document can call — because a `wire:navigate` takes those classes away with the\n
               body it replaces, and only [39m[31m<head>[39m[31m survives to be re-run, which it never is. So after\n
               the first navigation in a session the panel came back light under a dark theme and\n
               the rail came back wide. The body twin below calls this again on every navigation:\n
               the same arithmetic, not a second rule to keep in step.\n
\n
               `theme` and the layout are passed in rather than captured, because the body script\n
               re-runs with the values of the page that has just arrived. */\n
            window.adminShellSeed = function (theme, navTop) {\n
                const prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;\n
\n
                // Apply class immediately to prevent FOUT\n
                document.documentElement.classList.toggle(\n
                    'dark',\n
                    theme === 'dark' || (theme === 'auto' && prefersDark)\n
                );\n
\n
                // Restore the desktop sidebar rail width before paint, so the rail\n
                // does not re-widen on every full-page admin navigation.\n
                document.documentElement.classList.toggle(\n
                    'sidebar-collapsed',\n
                    localStorage.getItem('sidebarCollapsed') === 'true'\n
                );\n
\n
                // Same reason, different key: the keyboard badge has to know whether it is\n
                // writing ⌘ or Ctrl before the first frame, or every Mac user watches the\n
                // wrong glyph swap itself out a beat after the bar appears.\n
                document.documentElement.classList.toggle(\n
                    'os-mac',\n
                    /Mac|iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgentData?.platform || navigator.platform || '')\n
                );\n
\n
                // Which navigation the wide screen wears. Absent means the sidebar, so the default\n
                // layout needs no class of its own and nothing here has to know how many layouts\n
                // exist.\n
                document.documentElement.classList.toggle('admin-nav-top', navTop === true);\n
            };\n
\n
            const theme = "auto";\n
\n
            window.adminShellSeed(theme, false);\n
\n
            // Sync both our key and Flux's key so they stay in agreement\n
            localStorage.setItem('theme', theme);\n
            localStorage.setItem('flux.appearance', theme === 'auto' ? 'system' : theme);\n
        })();\n
    [39m[31m</script>[39m[31m\n

        \n
        [39m[31m<section class="hb-band hb-notes" aria-labelledby="notes-title" data-dash-reveal>[39m[31m\n
            [39m[31m<h2 class="hb-subhead" id="notes-title">[39m[31mHow this page works[39m[31m</h2>[39m[31m\n
            [39m[31m<ul>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m<li>[39m[31mEach card is measured and cached on its own, so one unavailable service cannot blank the page.[39m[31m</li>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m<li>[39m[31mCards report how long they took to measure; anything that touches the file system is warmed in the background instead of slowing a request.[39m[31m</li>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m<li>[39m[31mRe-measuring clears every cached card and reads the services again.[39m[31m</li>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m<li>[39m[31mConfiguration values are read from the running app, never from [39m[31m<code>[39m[31m.env[39m[31m</code>[39m[31m, so secrets stay out of the browser.[39m[31m</li>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m<li>[39m[31mExporting writes a redacted copy: lines a probe marks sensitive, such as the database endpoint, are left out and every download is recorded in the audit trail.[39m[31m</li>[39m[31m\n
            [39m[31m</ul>[39m[31m\n
        [39m[31m</section>[39m[31m\n
    [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
            [39m[31m</main>[39m[31m\n
        [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
    [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
\n
    \n
    [39m[31m<div\n
    x-data="{\n
    toasts: [],\n
    addToast(type, message, duration = 5000) {\n
        const id = Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);\n
        // Unshift to put newest toasts at the top\n
        this.toasts.unshift({ id, type, message, progress: 100, paused: false, duration });\n
        \n
        // Individual auto-dismiss based on variable duration\n
        setTimeout(() =>[39m[31m {\n
            this.dismissToast(id);\n
        }, duration);\n
    },\n
    dismissToast(id) {\n
        const index = this.toasts.findIndex(t => t.id === id);\n
        if (index !== -1) {\n
            this.toasts.splice(index, 1);\n
        }\n
    },\n
    init() {\n
                                        \n
        // Listen for Livewire-dispatched toast events (from PHP components).\n
        // The site and auth layouts render this component without Livewire on the page.\n
        if (typeof Livewire !== 'undefined') {\n
            Livewire.on('toast', (data) => {\n
                this.addToast(data.type, data.message, data.duration || 5000);\n
            });\n
        }\n
    }\n
}"\n
    @toast.window="addToast($event.detail.type, $event.detail.message, $event.detail.duration || 5000)"\n
    class="pointer-events-none fixed top-6 right-6 z-[9999] flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col gap-3"\n

    [39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
        document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
            Alpine.store('mobileMenu', {\n
                open: false,\n
\n
                toggle() {\n
                    this.open ? this.close() : this.show();\n
                },\n
\n
                show() {\n
                    this.open = true;\n
                    this.lock();\n
                    document.getElementById('sidebar-close-btn')?.focus();\n
                },\n
\n
                close() {\n
                    if (! this.open) {\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    this.open = false;\n
                    this.lock(false);\n
\n
                    // Hand focus back to the control that opened the drawer, so Tab does\n
                    // not restart at the top of the document.\n
                    document.getElementById('mobile-menu-btn')?.focus();\n
                },\n
\n
                // Content scrolls inside .admin-main, not the window, so the backdrop has\n
                // to suspend that scroller — locking [39m[31m<body>[39m[31m would do nothing at all.\n
                lock(locked = true) {\n
                    document.querySelector('.admin-main')?.classList.toggle('is-scroll-locked', locked);\n
                },\n
            });\n
\n
            Alpine.store('sidebar', {\n
                // Seeded from the [39m[31m<html>[39m[31m class applied before paint, so the two\n
                // can never disagree on first render.\n

                toggle() {\n
                    this.collapsed = !this.collapsed;\n

                },\n
            });\n
